#### 研究背景与动机

《Zero-Shot VLA Adaptation with Retrieved Guidance》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：MetaWorld, RA-VLA, Real-World, Real-to-Sim。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Pre-training large parametric "Vision-Language-Action" (VLA) models on large demonstration datasets has recently yielded promising results for general-purpose manipulation. However, these models still struggle under even small domain shifts relative to the training data, such as changes in camera viewpoint, background, object identity, or robot morphology. Motivated by the success of Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) in language models, we pursue retrieval to boost VLA performance. Specifically, we seek to leverage retrieval from domain-mismatched, "in-the-wild" demonstration datasets like DROID to guide a VLA's behavior in a new target domain. To achieve this, we introduce RAGu, which overcomes robotics-specific challenges for RAG: (1) It incorporates simple geometric calibration to transform source demonstrations into the target setup and reduce cross-domain differences. (2) It uses a coarse-to-fine retrieval strategy that selects, from a large pool of pre-warped candidates, the trajectory most compatible with the base VLA. (3) It guides the VLA's flow-matching inference procedure rather than seeking to retrain the base model or expand its limited context window. We evaluate RAGu across real-world, simulation, sim-to-real transfer, and cross-embodiment humanoid manipulation. We find it improves real-world VLA success rates from 20% to 80% and outperforms the closest-related baselines in sim. Project website: raguvla.github.io

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《DreamVLA: From Generated Human Motions to Humanoid Vision-Language-Action Policies》归入 具身智能、机器人、强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：DreamVLA, OpenVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Training vision-language-action (VLA) policies typically depends on substantial robot demonstration data, making human teleoperation a costly and cumbersome bottleneck, particularly for whole-body humanoid interaction. Reinforcement learning in simulation offers an alternative for scaling this data, but designing task-specific rewards remains challenging. To address this, we propose DreamVLA, which uses generated motion trajectories to guide reinforcement learning (RL) in simulation, converting them into reliable task execution trajectories. These trajectories train VLA models in simulation without new task-specific teleoperation data. Our central contribution is a reference-conditioned DAgger distillation procedure: after a failed learner rollout, we restart the RL expert from a sampled learner state with the same reference suffix and aggregate successful corrective trajectories, addressing the distribution shift left by offline imitation. We evaluate picking from a platform, opening a drawer, pressing a button, and walking and picking from a platform, with ablations of DAgger iterations, residual RL, and generated dataset scale. Project website: ember-center-berkeley.github.io/DreamVLA.

</details>

![[assets/2611.10769_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.10769](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10769)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.10769
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Look_Ahead_Search-Driven_Reasoning_in_Embodied_AI|Look Ahead: Search-Driven Reasoning in Embodied AI]]
- **作者**: Ahmed Zeer, Osama Shbib, Muzaffer Kaan Yuce, Himmet Toprak Kesgin, Mehmet Fatih Amasyali
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI
- **归属领域**: 具身智能

#### 研究背景与动机

《Look Ahead: Search-Driven Reasoning in Embodied AI》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；实时应用对效率提出要求。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：SmolVLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Search allows a robotic policy to evaluate candidate futures before committing to an action. Motivated by test-time planning for vision-language action (VLA) models, we study selective search over action chunks in PushT using a frozen Action Chunking with Transformers (ACT) policy. Beam search generates perturbed alternatives around the policy prediction and scores them through rollouts from cloned simulator states. Across 50 evaluation episodes, beam search raises task success from 0% to 18% and increases average cumulative reward from 39.44 to 103.2. We introduce One Step Ahead (OSA), a gate that simulates the nominal policy chunk and invokes full search only when its predicted coverage drop exceeds a threshold. In the reported ablations, OSA preserves or improves success while reducing runtime by approximately 3x relative to full beam search. Ablations over tree depth, branching factor, beam width, and replanning frequency show that larger search budgets do not consistently improve performance. The results support selective test-time search for action-chunking policies; evaluation on language-conditioned VLAs and physical robots remains future work.

</details>

- **arXiv**: [2611.10647](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10647)

#### 研究背景与动机

《Which Taxels, Not How Many: Tactile Sensor Placement for Dexterous Policies》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：围绕策略学习或控制策略展开；使用优化建模或搜索过程。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Policies for dexterous manipulation depend on design choices made before training: which sensors enter the observation, where they are placed, and how their readings are arranged for the encoder. These choices are rarely examined. For tactile sensing, simulation studies place taxels uniformly over the hand, and this placement has become a default without being tested. We test this default under a single controlled protocol. The protocol holds the teacher, the training procedure, and the candidate taxels fixed and varies only the sensor configuration and the representation. We evaluate it on in-palm rotation and grasping with three robot hands. A small set of taxels selected from contact statistics matches a far larger uniform set. Taxels at rarely contacted sites are equivalent to no tactile input. With all candidate taxels, their layout in the input tensor does not affect performance, even for a convolutional encoder. Uniform placement therefore overestimates the number of taxels a task requires. We distill these results into lessons for designing tactile policies and into a minimal set of design choices that a tactile policy study should report.

</details>

《ReSkill: Selective Self-Improvement for Robot Manipulation》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Self-improvement on compositional manipulation tasks requires deciding what to reuse, where to improve, and which changes to retain. We present ReSkill, a selective self-improvement framework that concentrates revision on capability gaps. A coding agent constructs an object-centric task graph from an instruction and a skill library, then uses node-level execution records to locate failures. At the task level, it revises stage organization, hand-off conditions, and recovery routes; at the skill level, it strengthens perception, motion, manipulation, and verification capabilities that repeatedly fail. Candidate revisions undergo full-task evaluation and paired comparisons before adoption, allowing local changes to accumulate into validated skills and an independently executable policy. The agent participates only during development; the deployed policy calls the retained skills without invoking the agent at run time. On the same development set, average success across ten RoboDojo tasks rises from 30.0% for the initial versions to 86.2% for the final adopted programs; retained programs achieve 83.6% in recorded robustness and model-selection evaluations. Across eight real-world tasks, ReSkill succeeds in 72 of 80 rebuilt-simulation trials and 62 of 80 real-robot trials (77.5%). The median revision changes only three functions, approximately 0.5% of a program, while 39% of task-specific functions in later-added tasks directly reuse existing implementations. These results show how locating local deficiencies, validating candidate changes, and accumulating reusable skills can improve execution of compositional manipulation tasks.

</details>

![[assets/2611.10443_figure.png|800]]

- **作者**: Jungin Park, Chaoran Zhu, Changjae Oh, Kwanghoon Sohn

#### 研究背景与动机

《Spatial Representation Distillation and Knowledge Routing for Vision-Language-Action Models》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Real-World, SPARK-VLA。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Understanding three-dimensional physical structure has become essential for vision-language-action models (VLAs). However, existing approaches either rely on explicit geometric inputs at inference time or adopt a single visual pathway, potentially compromising the visual-semantic knowledge inherited from pre-trained models. We present SPARK-VLA, Spatial Representation distillation and Knowledge routing for VLAs, a preserve-specialize-route framework for spatially adapting VLAs without requiring external 3D inputs or experts at inference. SPARK-VLA consists of two key components: (1) spatial representation distillation, which transfers 3D understanding from a frozen 3D expert to a LoRA-adapted visual branch by aligning attention responses while preserving the original visual pathway, and (2) a lightweight knowledge router, which selectively forwards action-relevant visual tokens to the language model using routing supervision derived from action-loss gradients. Across LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, CALVIN, and real-world manipulation, SPARK-VLA achieves strong performance with only a 0.5B language backbone and approximately 53M trainable parameters, providing an effective and parameter-efficient approach to 3D-aware VLA learning. Code is available at https://github.com/park-jungin/spark-vla.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/SimDuoReal_Object-Centric_Bimanual_Policy_for_Emergent_Dexterity|SimDuoReal: Object-Centric Bimanual Policy for Emergent Dexterity]]

![[assets/2611.10410_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.10410](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10410)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.10410
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/SimDuoReal_Object-Centric_Bimanual_Policy_for_Emergent_Dexterity|SimDuoReal: Object-Centric Bimanual Policy for Emergent Dexterity]]
- **作者**: Qiyang Yan, Yulin Liu, Akash Sharma, Haozhi Qi, Guanya Shi, C. Karen Liu
- **cs 子类**: cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 具身智能
- **相关领域**: 具身智能, 机器人, 强化学习

#### 研究背景与动机

《SimDuoReal: Object-Centric Bimanual Policy for Emergent Dexterity》归入 具身智能、机器人、强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Real-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Bimanual dexterous manipulation requires coordinated arm and hand control, yet specifying such coordination manually is challenging and often task-specific. In this work, we explore whether complex bimanual dexterity can emerge from a simple object-centric learning objective. We train reinforcement learning policies under a shared object-centric reward, varying their training environments rather than tuning separate rewards for each behavior. We find that the policies discover pre-grasp manipulation, grasping and regrasping, in-hand adjustment and handover to reach object-pose goals without hand-designed motion sequences. We then refine the experts on transition states and use DAgger and reinforcement learning to distill them into a single policy that composes these behaviors for diverse bimanual tasks. On real hardware, the same policy succeeds in 61 of 80 trials (76.3\%) across four tasks with unseen objects and no task-specific fine-tuning. In simulated barcode scanning, our policy achieves 85.0\% success, compared with 60.4\% for two single-arm policies without handover. These results suggest that this objective provides a simple and scalable interface for learning general bimanual dexterity, shifting the burden from manually designing robot coordination to specifying what should happen to the object.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《CPNav: Calibrated Vision-Language Navigation for Object-Goal Search in Unseen Environments》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Directing a robot to locate a target object in unseen environments remains a central challenge in embodied AI. Vision-Language Model (VLM)-based methods provide rich semantic reasoning but often produce uncalibrated and overconfident action decisions. We introduce CPNav, a navigation framework that combines collision-aware planning with statistically calibrated VLM action scoring for map-free object-goal navigation. From RGB-D input, CPNav constructs a navigability map, generates collision-free motion primitives, and queries two VLMs for goal detection and action scoring. The resulting scores are calibrated offline via conformal prediction (CP) using oracle actions obtained through a tree-structured exploration procedure with backtracking, providing finite-sample guarantees at a user-specified risk level. The CP layer is model-agnostic and can wrap any VLM policy. Online, calibrated VLM scores guide action selection, while a tree-structured exploration history records alternative actions and allows backtracking. We evaluate CPNav in both simulation and real-world hardware settings. In AI Habitat simulation, compared with the uncalibrated baseline method, CPNav achieves relative SR and SPL improvements of 6.2% and 11.4% on HM3D v0.2, and 12.4% and 8.8% on MP3D, respectively. In real-world deployment on a modified Hiwonder MentorPi robot, the corresponding improvements in SR and SPL are 24.9% and 15.2%, respectively. Code is available at https://github.com/practice-lab-ucla/CPnav.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《GUARD: Anomaly-Informed Q-Steering for Generative Robot Policies in Dexterous Manipulation》归入 具身智能、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Real-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Generative robot policies based on diffusion and flow matching can model complex, multimodal behaviors, but remain susceptible to covariate shift during closed-loop execution. Existing approaches mitigate such failures through additional demonstrations, policy fine-tuning, or test-time guidance, but often require human intervention, engineered rewards, or other task-specific signals. We introduce GUARD, an anomaly-informed Q-steering framework that leverages the policy's training distribution to construct a dense, failure-anticipatory steering signal. GUARD pairs sparse task rewards with dense anomaly scores learned from policy training data and propagates both through a learned Q-function. Its gradients then steer the denoising process toward actions that improve task return while preventing trajectories from entering poorly supported regions, without directly modifying the pretrained policy. Across four Adroit dexterous manipulation tasks, GUARD achieves average success rates of $96.0\%$ and $80.0\%$ under nominal and perturbed dynamics, outperforming the strongest baseline by $21.0$ and $20.0$ percentage points while maintaining the lowest anomaly score. GUARD further demonstrates direct sim-to-real transfer on in-hand screwdriver rotation and reorientation tasks, achieving $40$ and $25$ percentage-point gains in success rate. These results demonstrate that anomaly signals can serve not only for failure monitoring but also to complement sparse task rewards as effective supervision for robust policy steering.

</details>

《Capturability-Aware Footstep Planning in Cluttered Environments: A Recursively Feasible Sequential MPC on Axis-Aligned Safe Corridors》归入 具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：设计端到端框架；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开；使用优化建模或搜索过程。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

A fundamental aspect of humanoid footstep planning is to jointly guarantee obstacle avoidance and locomotion stability. This paper presents a capturability-aware sequential Model Predictive Control (MPC) framework for Linear Inverted Pendulum (LIP)-based humanoid footstep planning in the presence of obstacles. The proposed method combines obstacle avoidance with capturability constraints through safe corridors constructed as sequences of intersecting rectangles, yielding a sequential MPC framework with recursive feasibility guarantees for the LIP-based planning layer. The planner autonomously adapts to constrained scenarios, switching to in-place walking when obstructions---such as doors assumed to be open during planning but unexpectedly found to be closed during execution---block forward motion. Performance is largely independent of the prediction horizon, allowing high success rates while keeping the computational cost low. The approach is validated in high-fidelity simulations with the humanoid robot Digit, demonstrating a practical solution for capturable humanoid footstep planning in cluttered environments.

</details>

<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Learning humanoid state estimation and language-grounded motion understanding from robot recordings ties training coverage to the robot's motion repertoire. Human motion datasets offer broader coverage, yet transferring this knowledge requires overcoming differences in body geometry, sensing, and physical disturbances. To enable this transfer, we propose a unified inertial encoder whose shared inertial interface uses kinematic IMU synthesis to represent human and humanoid motion at corresponding body sites. Within this common input space, joint training on human and kinematically retargeted humanoid motion combines velocity estimation and motion--text alignment with continuous-time encoding and sensor and motion randomization. The resulting model transfers zero-shot to a physical Unitree G1 without physics simulation, real-robot training trajectories, or fine-tuning, achieving the lowest velocity error among evaluated methods across five datasets, including 0.099m/s on real G1. Its motion-description retrieval also extends to retargeted G1 motion categories observed only in human training, demonstrating how human motion knowledge can support the generalization of humanoid inertial odometry and language-grounded motion understanding.

</details>

#### 实验结果分析

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Pretrained vision language action (VLA) models show strong zero-shot performance, but are rarely deployed directly and are often first post-trained on demonstrations from the target setting. Yet VLAs are typically compared by zero-shot or full-data fine-tuned success, revealing little about the data and compute required for adaptation. We study adaptation efficiency for six VLAs ($π_{0}$, $π_{0.5}$, and GR00T N1-N1.7) on four LIBERO suites, varying both demonstrations and training steps. This reveals differences hidden by full-data fine-tuning: models within two points on the full LIBERO-Spatial dataset differ by up to $3\times$ in success with 50 demonstrations. $π_{0.5}$ needs $2$--$5\times$ fewer demonstrations than $π_{0}$ and is the most sample-efficient model at every budget, with the largest gap on the long-horizon suite. Within GR00T, N1.5 adapts more efficiently than N1.6 and N1.7 on three of four suites, despite their stronger zero-shot performance. We study one pretraining factor behind these differences by freezing the VLM during finetuning: this severely degrades the $π$ models, whose VLM is jointly trained with the action expert during pretraining, but barely affects GR00T models. Finally, validation loss on held-out demonstrations tracks closed-loop success within a suite, but understates how much more sample-efficient $π_{0.5}$ is than $π_{0}$.

</details>

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Soft powered descent landings are becoming evermore important in the field of space exploration and scientific advancement, because of their ability to deliver mission critical payloads, both highly accurately and safely. The high efficiency restriction on planetary landings due to the severely limited fuel supply results in the need for optimal guidance trajectories and control. Model based reinforcement learning is proving to be an important tool in simplifying the highly complex process of generating optimal guidance trajectories and control. In this work, we present DreamLander, a state of the art model based reinforcement learning algorithm based on DreamerV3, to generate optimal guidance and control for a realistic 6 degree of freedom entry descent landing problem. The model based approach creates a sample efficient and highly generalizable model that can be applied to a variety of scenarios whilst maintaining a high degree of accuracy, landing within 2 meters of the designated landing zone, and with velocity norm less than 0.3 meters per second. DreamLander makes use of state sensor observations, making progress toward End-to-End solutions of the Entry Descent Landing problem.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Safe Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Turn Clinical Agents: When Dense Rewards Beat Outcome Rewards》归入 强化学习、大模型、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、大模型、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Pre-RL, SDBench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards is increasingly used to improve language model reasoning, yet its effects on sequential decision procedures remain poorly understood. We study this problem in clinical multi-turn agents that must decide what information to request, when to stop gathering evidence, and which action to take. We introduce three controlled environments: medication refill triage, acute diagnostic work-up, and dosing titration, each with hidden clinical states, query costs, exact adaptive oracles, and deterministic outcome verifiers. Across these tasks, outcome-only group relative policy optimization often improves aggregate reward while producing undesirable procedures, including premature commitment, one-question collapse, and failures on rare high-risk cases. In diagnostics, assigning dense credit to questions that reduce uncertainty improves held-out value and moderate-case support across three training seeds relative to outcome-only reinforcement learning, without the high-risk seed collapse observed under outcome-only training. Separating exploration from exploitation further increases high-risk support at its best checkpoint, but at the cost of excessive information gathering on low-acuity cases. Additional experiments show that the optimal strength of process credit is task-dependent; that after a supervised warmup that already gathers carefully, reinforcement learning raises aggregate reward by trimming questions while degrading high-risk performance; and that reinforcement learning does not reliably repair rare behavior gaps inherited from biased supervised data. Verifiable final outcomes are therefore insufficient for training reliable multi-turn agents: reward design must account for the procedure used to reach an answer, and evaluation must distinguish adaptive information gathering from reward-exploiting shortcuts.

</details>

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Reinforcement Learning (RL) algorithms often rely on data collected from a target environment, which can be expensive and noisy. Although simulators can generate large amounts of additional data, standard simulation mixing approaches, which directly combine simulated and real experience, can degrade performance due to simulator inaccuracies, especially when they bias the solution away from the optimal policy. We propose a conservative simulation-aided learning method based on control variates, where simulated data is used solely to reduce variance in the learning process rather than directly shaping the learning target. We develop our approach for Policy-Evaluation, which is a fundamental building block of RL algorithms. Then we combine it within the actor-critic framework towards policy improvement, as well as extend it towards offline RL scenarios. Experiments on multiple MuJoCo benchmark tasks demonstrate improvements over standard simulation mixing approaches, highlighting control variates as an effective tool for simulation-aided reinforcement learning.

</details>

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

User plane function (UPF) selection in 5G networks is an active area of research, with various limited approaches proposed to enhance network efficiency and manage resources effectively. In this paper, we address this gap in the related literature by proposing a Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) UPF selection method that utilizes a dynamic, multi-objective reward function and validating it on a real-world 5G emulation platform. The proposed method is based on the large language models' (LLMs) translation of network operator intents and the network data analytics function (NWDAF) abnormality alerts. The experimental results validated in the real-world experimental setup demonstrate that our dynamic DRL solution achieves an effective balance between latency, power consumption, and traffic distribution, outperforming the heuristic baselines and fixed DRL by 16.89%. These findings highlight the real-world applicability of our approach for dynamic, intent-driven UPF selection.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/Uncertainty-Guided_Reward_Labeling_for_Reinforcement_Learning_under_Limited_Feedback|Uncertainty-Guided Reward Labeling for Reinforcement Learning under Limited Feedback]]

![[assets/2611.11091_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.11091](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11091)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.11091
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/Uncertainty-Guided_Reward_Labeling_for_Reinforcement_Learning_under_Limited_Feedback|Uncertainty-Guided Reward Labeling for Reinforcement Learning under Limited Feedback]]
- **作者**: Renhao Zhang, Shreyas Chaudhari, Bruno Castro da Silva
- **cs 子类**: cs.LG
- **归属领域**: 强化学习
- **相关领域**: 强化学习, 世界模型, 大模型

#### 研究背景与动机

《Uncertainty-Guided Reward Labeling for Reinforcement Learning under Limited Feedback》归入 强化学习、世界模型、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、世界模型、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：IDRL, Offline-RL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

In reinforcement learning from limited feedback (RLLF), only a small fraction of an offline dataset can be labeled with rewards, and the central question is which samples should be labeled to learn a high-performing policy from the resulting partially labeled dataset. Prior work formalized this as a reward-selection problem by focusing on the selection stage while treating downstream policy learning as a black box, in a regime where queried rewards are not retained for reward-model training. We instead study the retained-label setting, where queried rewards can be stored and used to fit a reward model before policy learning. We bound the suboptimality of the learned policy in terms of two sources of error: one arising from offline RL on a fixed, offline dataset, and the other from uncertainty in the reward model. Since reward selection cannot change the offline dataset, the limited labeling budget must be used to strategically reduce reward uncertainty. Motivated by RLLF's observation that useful rewards tend to keep the agent on high-return trajectories, we introduce Successor-Guided Uncertainty REduction (SURE), a novel method that uses successor features to select rewards that are both uncertain and associated with states from which high-valued states are reachable. We formally derive SURE from a bound-induced design objective and characterize its exact one-step marginal gain. Empirically, SURE reaches near full-feedback performance with few reward labels across a variety of domains and offline-RL backbones, yielding an effective method for feedback-efficient reinforcement learning.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《DPDP: Diffusion Policy-Steered Diffusion Planner for Offline Reinforcement Learning》归入 强化学习、具身智能、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；引入智能体式建模或搜索；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、具身智能、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：D4RL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

In offline reinforcement learning, diffusion models fill two roles with complementary strengths. Typically guided by a critic, a diffusion policy is value-aware and favors high-return, behavior-supported actions, but it is short-sighted: it never lays out the states it intends to visit. A diffusion planner is far-sighted, synthesizing such a long-horizon trajectory, but it is only behavior-driven at execution, realizing each planned transition using only offline behavior.Each is strong exactly where the other is weak. We introduce DPDP to bring them together at the planner's execution step, where a planned transition becomes an action. Reading the planner's inverse-dynamics executor as a posterior over actions: the environment's transition likelihood relates an action to the planned next state, while the behavioral prior determines its weight under the offline data. Replacing this prior with a value-optimized diffusion policy preserves the planned transition likelihood and favors valuable actions that can serve the plan. We target the resulting clean-action distribution in two ways. A training-free sampler composes policy and inverse-dynamics scores at matched action noise levels and applies Langevin correction; this route uses a shared action noise schedule and an approximately common terminal prior. A preference objective instead amortizes the transfer into a single diffusion executor. Across D4RL, DPDP improves the planner it builds on, staying competitive with reactive diffusion policies on locomotion and leading on long-horizon navigation.

</details>

- **作者**: Jacky Chen, Ing-Haw Cheng, John Hull, Landis Li, Zissis Poulos, Justin Shi, Andreas Veneris, Zeyu Wang

- **归属领域**: 强化学习

- **关联关键词**: Agent, RL

#### 研究背景与动机

《Risk-Sensitive Reinforcement Learning for an Options Market Maker: An Empirical Study with Real S&P 500 Options》归入 强化学习、大模型、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入智能体式建模或搜索；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、大模型、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Options market makers absorb client order flow and must hedge the resulting inventory risk under trading frictions. We study whether an option hedging reinforcement learning (RL) agent trained on historical market data outperforms three alternatives in tail-risk protection: (i) traditional delta hedging, (ii) simple hedge-ratio rules, and (iii) the same agent trained in simulated markets. Most RL hedging agents are trained in model-generated markets that do not capture the crashes and volatility regimes present in real data. We introduce an empirical replay environment using S&amp;P 500 option data from 1996 to 2017 and compare against a matched geometric Brownian motion (GBM) environment, a stochastic-volatility (SABR) simulator, and a hybrid that values historical paths with Black-Scholes, all sharing the same portfolio mechanics, stochastic arrivals, costs, and test period. We train a distributional Twin Delayed Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (TD3) agent and evaluate it out of sample over 2018-2023. The agent trained on empirical data reduces Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR) tail loss by 3.1% compared to delta hedging and outperforms every hedge-ratio rule chosen during the training period and every alternative training environment. The reduction reaches 8.9% in volatile and falling markets, but reverses to a cost of 7-10% in rising markets. Empirical training induces a state-dependent short net delta: the policy under-hedges negative-delta books, most strongly after prices have fallen. This tilt disappears with GBM training and is weaker with SABR or hybrid training. These findings suggest that exposure to historical market dynamics can materially change a tail-risk hedging policy, although the evidence rests on a single test path and the edge is regime-conditional.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Learning When to Think: Adaptive Internal Computation for Reinforcement Learning》归入 强化学习、机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：PonderNet。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Iterative neural policies usually spend the same computation on every decision, although the benefit of additional processing varies across states. We introduce Adaptive Internal Computation (AIC), an iterative policy that learns when to stop refining its latent representation and act. In AIC, we train action selection and stopping jointly, an expected-depth penalty values computation and intermediate value predictions provide auxiliary supervision. We show that when only some states benefit from further refinement, an ideal state-dependent allocator outperforms every state-independent stopping schedule at the same expected depth. With at most eight refinement steps per decision, AIC executes 3.3 steps on average across twelve benchmark tasks and lowers per-decision latency by 31--44% relative to a fixed eight-step policy on four control and robotics tasks. Its mean reward exceeds the best fixed-depth policy on seven tasks and trails it on four. Ablating adaptive stopping and value supervision separately shows that stopping accounts for most of the reward gain on PickCube and AntMaze, where it also executes more refinement steps and that supervision accounts for most of it on RareEvent.

</details>

- **作者**: Chenglong Wu, Yi Zhou, Bin Cheng, Nan Zhang, Hexi Baoyin
- **cs 子类**: 
- **归属领域**: 强化学习

#### 研究背景与动机

《Deep Reinforcement Learning with Massively Parallel Simulation for Low-Thrust Multi-Revolution Rendezvous》归入 强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 cs.OH 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：DRL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

The growing demand for on-orbit servicing and constellation management in low Earth orbit requires autonomous and reliable low-thrust rendezvous. Traditional numerical methods struggle with the long-horizon nature of multi-revolution maneuvers under perturbations, while standard deep reinforcement learning (DRL) methods are limited by low simulation throughput and poor sample efficiency. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a massively parallel DRL guidance framework. A fully tensorized, GPU-resident orbital propagator is developed to integrate $J_2$-perturbed orbital dynamics and policy optimization within a zero-copy pipeline, achieving up to a 67-fold increase in simulation throughput over conventional CPU baselines. In addition, the rendezvous problem is formulated in modified equinoctial elements and trained with a progressive curriculum learning strategy to improve convergence and generalization across increasingly challenging rendezvous scenarios. Simulations show that the proposed framework can learn robust rendezvous trajectories for complex multi-revolution rendezvous over a broad range of orbital configurations. Additional evaluations under a high-fidelity model and eclipse-induced thrust interruptions further demonstrate the robustness of the learned policy under practical mission constraints. Moreover, the trajectories generated by the proposed framework can be used as effective initial guesses for optimization-based trajectory solvers, improving the convergence of high-precision trajectory optimization. The method offers a scalable approach for real-time low-thrust guidance and trajectory optimization.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《SLA-Aware Task Offloading in the IoT Edge-Cloud Continuum: Class-Weighted Deep Reinforcement Learning Against an Exact Optimum》归入 强化学习、大模型、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入智能体式建模或搜索；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、大模型、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；关注鲁棒性或泛化表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Deep reinforcement learning (RL) is often used to decide where Internet of Things (IoT) tasks with different latency limits run in the edge-cloud continuum, yet some methods give every missed task the same penalty and few compare their agents with an optimal policy. This benchmark study builds four offloading systems small enough to solve exactly by dynamic programming and measures reward and learner against the optimum. A bonus for a met limit makes each miss cost its penalty plus the bonus, which shifts the class weights of published per-class rewards. A class-blind penalty stays 65% to 118% above the optimal cost even with a perfect learner, while a corrected per-class penalty comes within 0.13% of the optimum. With a per-class reward, tuned proximal policy optimization (PPO) agents come within 9% to 11% of the optimum in the smallest system, and the corrected one beats a tuned reservation rule in 9 of 10 seeds. In larger systems the agents stay 41% to 76% above the optimum, and a tuned rule beats their mean. Training ten times longer does not close this gap, and a PPO-Lagrangian agent with reachable targets does no better than the per-class reward.

</details>

- **作者**: Bo Yang, Yuebing Liang, Zhan Zhao, Fangyi Ding, Yunhan Zheng, Shenhao Wang, Jinhua Zhao
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.LG
- **归属领域**: 强化学习

#### 研究背景与动机

《Transit-Oriented Land Use Planning Using Deep Reinforcement Learning》归入 强化学习、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：DRL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Transit-Oriented Development (TOD) is a key strategy for advancing sustainable urban development by promoting high-density, mixed-use land development within walking distance of public transit stations. However, traditional TOD planning often relies on heuristic methods, making it difficult to efficiently identify quantitatively optimized transit-oriented land use patterns. This paper presents DRL4TOD, a deep reinforcement learning framework that automatically optimizes land use types and floor area ratios (FARs) around transit stations in alignment with TOD principles. DRL4TOD incorporates a graph neural network to capture spatial interdependencies among land parcels, road networks, and metro stations, and uses a DRL-based optimization strategy to assign land-use types and FARs to selected parcels. It jointly optimizes multiple objectives, including land use density, land use diversity, and transit capacity utilization, while minimizing reconstruction costs to account for development feasibility. Applied to two real-world station areas in Shanghai, DRL4TOD consistently outperforms rule-based approaches and genetic algorithms, achieving more effective TOD outcomes with lower reconstruction costs. By adjusting objective weights, the framework enables flexible prioritization, such as placing greater emphasis on cost minimization when financial feasibility is a concern. These results demonstrate DRL4TOD's potential as a decision-support tool for generating coordinated land-use and FAR configurations under planner-specified priorities, rather than prescribing a single fixed design. For policymakers, the framework provides parcel-level, constraint-aware alternatives that make trade-offs among competing objectives explicit and support targeted decisions about development intensity and feasibility.

</details>

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：ShiftWM-Bench, SyncWorld。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

A world model predicts how a scene changes after an action, and a planner uses it to search for actions that reach a goal. If the camera moves or the controller changes, the same command moves the robot differently: when its commands are turned by 90 degrees, a frozen planner with the LeWM world model that succeeds in 77.5% of episodes succeeds in only 7.8%. Current fixes retrain the model, need training data, or forget a set-up when it returns. We propose Library of Hands (LoH), a training-free method that keeps the world model frozen and corrects only the map from commands to applied actions, which we call the hand. LoH reads the hand through the frozen model, with no extra sensor: it finds the action that best explains the change between two camera images (a reading). It stores every hand it identifies, so a returning set-up is recognised rather than re-learnt. In closed-loop tests, LoH more than doubles success on three simulated tasks, from 28.6% to 62.3%, and loses only 0.6 percentage points when nothing changes. It also works, unchanged, with two other world models and on two of three models pretrained on real robot data, and its gain grows with the quality of its readings.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/世界模型/How_Many_Exposures_Does_a_World_Model_Need_Learning_Unseen_Game_Mechanics_at_Test_Time|How Many Exposures Does a World Model Need? Learning Unseen Game Mechanics at Test Time]]

![[assets/2611.12324_figure.png|800]]

- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.CV, cs.LG

#### 研究背景与动机

《How Many Exposures Does a World Model Need? Learning Unseen Game Mechanics at Test Time》归入 世界模型、大模型、强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、大模型、强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现；关注鲁棒性或泛化表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：AdaWorld, AutumnBench, MechEval, ScratchWorld。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

World models enable agents to plan and learn from imagined experience, but in an unfamiliar environment they must learn its mechanics from limited interaction. A mechanic is a rule for how an action or an interaction changes part of the environment. Each occurrence of a mechanic that the model observes is one exposure. We ask how many exposures a world model needs to predict the effects of a new environment's mechanics. We address this question on ARC-AGI-3, a benchmark of interactive games defined by their programs. We introduce MechEval, which labels 69 recurring mechanics across 20 games from these programs. It scores each prediction on the cells the mechanic changes at each exposure. As a test bed, we build WARC (World model for ARC-AGI-3), a 35M-parameter vision transformer that is pretrained on other games and updates all of its parameters after each transition. On held-out games, WARC learns to predict four in five mechanics with at least 80% accuracy on the cells they change and keeps that accuracy to the end of the recordings. Half of them reach that accuracy within about 80 exposures, in a single play of the game, and without updates only one in seven does at all. In closed-loop play on the public games, WARC-Agent, which pairs WARC with a fixed graph explorer, triples the explorer's official efficiency score from 0.15 to 0.46, mostly through test-time training. On the hidden games of the Kaggle leaderboard, our entry scores 0.20.

</details>

- **作者**: Xian Yeow Lee, Ryoichi Takase, Ahmed Farahat
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.LG
- **归属领域**: 世界模型
- **相关领域**: 世界模型, 强化学习, 具身智能, 机器人, 大模型

#### 研究背景与动机

《An Evaluation of Tabular Foundation Models as Training-Free World Models》归入 世界模型、强化学习、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、强化学习、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：D4RL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

World models are widely considered to be a potential key component of model-based reinforcement learning and robot planning, but typically require task-specific training on substantial interaction data. We investigate whether a pretrained tabular foundation model (TabFM) can serve as a training-free dynamics model for world modeling by framing next-state prediction as in-context regression over transition pairs, and where such a model helps and where it breaks. Across synthetic dynamical systems and robot locomotion benchmarks, TabFM is more accurate than LSTM and MLP models trained from scratch on the same limited transitions in almost all settings, and among regressors fitted to the same data it is the most accurate on locomotion, while a Gaussian process is more accurate on smooth low-dimensional systems. However, models trained on the full dataset remain substantially better on most systems. When rolled out autoregressively over multiple steps, TabFM's accuracy depends strongly on how much context it is given. With a short context, its rollouts quickly degrade to the level of a trivial baseline, whereas with a long context they improve substantially and, on one locomotion task, exceed a trained LSTM. Inference is also about three orders of magnitude slower than an LSTM. Overall, in their current state, we find that training-free priors are useful as predictors when data are scarce and enough context is available, but they are not yet a substitute for trained world models in planning.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《How Many Candidates Does a One-Step World Model Need? A Random-Codebook Account of Nearest-of-m Training on Frozen DINO Tokens》归入 世界模型、机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

One-step stochastic world models avoid the mode averaging of deterministic feature predictors without the tens of network evaluations per step that diffusion and flow matching require. The simplest such objective is nearest-of-m matching (conditional implicit maximum likelihood estimation, IMLE): draw m candidate futures and pull only the one closest to the observed outcome. Recent work conjectures that a small m suffices for feature-space world models and that a strictly proper energy score should replace the hard minimum. We test both claims with one-step world models on frozen DINOv2/v3 patch tokens, using benchmarks whose futures can be enumerated. Under mode-like uncertainty, nearest-of-m with m=16 covers the enumerated futures better than 50-step flow matching (at about 1/30 of its latency), one-step flow generators and an energy-score generator at matched parameter counts, and its rollouts stay on the token manifold where flow rollouts drift. One quantity governs the choice of m: the nearest-of-m objective is the expected distortion of a random codebook of m draws from the generator. This view yields three predictions, recorded before the corresponding runs: (i) memorization of the realized outcome in contexts that never repeat, whose strength is governed by the codebook's miss distortion I(m) rather than its miss probability, and which an energy-score generator does not escape by raising m; (ii) a flattening of mode probabilities with m, matching a known optimum within 0.01; and (iii) under-dispersion on continuous high-dimensional outcomes, which bounds the method on real robot video. Per-step matching on self-rollouts cuts long-horizon error by a third on a stochastic Push-T variant; an ablation separates its manifold and coverage benefits. Planning success does not improve despite accurate risk estimates, a negative result we report with its diagnosis.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/世界模型/Planners_Exploit_Deterministic_Error_Dithered_Low-Bit_World_Models|Planners Exploit Deterministic Error: Dithered Low-Bit World Models]]

![[assets/2611.11780_first_page.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.11780](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11780)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.11780

- **相关领域**: 世界模型, 机器人, 具身智能, 强化学习

- **关联关键词**: Agent, Robotics, WorldModel

#### 研究背景与动机

《Planners Exploit Deterministic Error: Dithered Low-Bit World Models》归入 世界模型、机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Meta-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Sampling-based planners such as MPPI and CEM query a learned world model thousands of times per control step, so low-bit world models are an obvious way to make model-based control cheaper on a robot. With round-to-nearest activation quantization and no calibration, we find that planning can collapse, by up to 100 points of success, while the world model's latent one-step prediction error stays no larger than that of a stochastic rounding scheme that plans well. At the same bit width, what decides the outcome is not the size of the rounding error but whether it persists across planner iterations and is shared across candidates. Round-to-nearest leaves an error that persists, and an iterating planner drifts toward the candidates that this error favors; in TD-MPC2, holding the same dither fixed within a step or an episode brings the drift back. Dither shared by all candidates and redrawn at every iteration removes most of the damage at the same bit width and without training: at least half of it in all 19 affected Meta-World cells (task $\times$ bit width) of TD-MPC2 (median 95%), in 4 of 5 DINO-WM cells, and in all 8 affected tasks of a 317M-parameter multi-task TD-MPC2. Captured as CUDA graphs on an Ampere GPU, a W8A4 integer TD-MPC2 world model runs its planning rollouts 1.9-3.3$\times$ and the whole planning step 1.3-1.5$\times$ faster than FP32, though not faster than BF16 with pre-converted weights; shared dither adds at most 2.3% latency. In TD-MPC2, block-scaled NVFP4 removes most of the damage and MXFP4 part of it, which ties the failure to coarse grids with a shared scale. Hypotheses, thresholds and analysis code were fixed before the data that test them, and the hypotheses that failed are reported.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/世界模型/K-PWM_Control-Oriented_Structured_Bilinear_World_Models_under_Partial_Observation|K-PWM: Control-Oriented Structured Bilinear World Models under Partial Observation]]

![[assets/2611.11521_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2611.11521](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11521)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2611.11521
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/世界模型/K-PWM_Control-Oriented_Structured_Bilinear_World_Models_under_Partial_Observation|K-PWM: Control-Oriented Structured Bilinear World Models under Partial Observation]]
- **作者**: Santosh Mohan Rajkumar, Sriram Narayanan, Samuel E. Otto, Debdipta Goswami
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.LG, cs.RO
- **归属领域**: 世界模型
- **相关领域**: 世界模型, 强化学习
- **相关性评分**: 1.52（加权：强化学习 0.16，世界模型 1.36）
- **关联关键词**: Agent, WorldModel, Systems

#### 研究背景与动机

《K-PWM: Control-Oriented Structured Bilinear World Models under Partial Observation》归入 世界模型、强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：给出系统化方法或工具；引入智能体式建模或搜索；使用优化建模或搜索过程。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：MBRL, MFRL, PlaNet。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

World models learn internal simulators of environment dynamics for prediction, planning, and control, yet learning them from limited, noisy, and partial observations remains challenging. While much progress has focused on pixel-based observations, many physical systems instead provide vector-valued measurements that reveal only part of the underlying state. We introduce K-PWM, a probabilistic world model for prediction and control under partial observation. K-PWM couples Koopman-bilinear latent dynamics with a nonlinear observation decoder and performs probabilistic latent-state inference through the learned model via extended Kalman filtering and smoothing, without a separately trained inference network. Its bilinear structure enables exact propagation of the maintained Gaussian belief through the latent transition and efficient rollouts for uncertainty-aware model predictive path integral (MPPI) control. For training K-PWM, we present a generalized expectation conditional maximization (GECM) procedure with closed-form updates for the generators, noise covariances, and latent prior, complemented by gradient-based decoder learning. A Koopman-consistent initialization selects the latent dimension from data and initializes the action-dependent dynamics. With limited training data, K-PWM achieves lower long-horizon prediction error than the evaluated baselines on partially observed Gymnasium MuJoCo systems, while substantially reducing training time relative to the strongest prediction baseline. On high-dimensional MuJoCo Menagerie robots, it achieves competitive tracking accuracy with low per-step computation times, demonstrating potential for real-time deployment. These results support structured probabilistic dynamics as a data- and compute-efficient approach to world modeling and control under partial observation.

</details>

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：FoldingNet, PointNet。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Estimating the motion of deformable bodies in soft robotics from proprioceptive sensing is challenging due to partial observability, as internal observations provide only indirect access to global motion, contact, and shape evolution. In such settings, instantaneous observations are insufficient, and accurate estimation requires temporally consistent latent representations. This paper studies deformable motion estimation under proprioceptive partial observability from a latent dynamics perspective. We argue that a key requirement for long-horizon estimation is latent temporal consistency, where latent states remain predictive of future observations. To this end, we present DeepSoMo, a learning-based framework that enforces predictive structure in latent dynamics through a self-supervised latent prediction constraint. We evaluate the approach in a canonical deformable dynamics setting using high-fidelity finite-element simulation as a controlled source of ground truth. Results demonstrate consistent improvements in trajectory estimation, orientation recovery, shape reconstruction, and contact inference over architecture-matched recurrent baselines.

</details>

《ObjectWorld: Scaling Object-Centric World Models with A Large-Scale 3D Full-Object Flow Dataset》归入 世界模型、具身智能、机器人 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、具身智能、机器人 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Meta-World, ObjectWorld, PointWorld, RLBench, Real-World, Track4World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Generative world modeling from large-scale visual data offers a promising path toward scalable robot manipulation. This calls for an intermediate representation that can be supervised at scale and remains actionable for downstream control. We propose 3D full-object flow, an object-centric motion representation that assigns dense 3D trajectories to points across the complete surface of a target object, including its occluded regions. By extending visible-surface object flow to the full object geometry, this representation makes complete object motion explicit and applies uniformly to both rigid and deformable objects. To realize the potential of this representation at scale, we introduce a 1210-hour full-object flow dataset that addresses the scarcity of large-scale supervision for complete 3D object motion. The dataset integrates diverse real-world videos and physical simulations, with a scalable annotation pipeline for recovering full-object motion from real-world observations and quality metrics for systematically assessing annotation quality. We then train a world model that takes an image and a task description as input and predicts dense full-object flow over the reconstructed object geometry. Experiments show reliable annotation quality, data-scalable flow prediction, and effective downstream manipulation across novel scenes and robot embodiments. Project website with additional results and videos: https://nus-lins-lab.github.io/ObjectWorld/.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《The World Model Planning Accelerator (WMPA): Hardware-Algorithm Co-Design for Embedded AI in Robotics》归入 世界模型、机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；实时应用对效率提出要求。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Embedded AI is moving from perception to prediction. Robots now use world models$\unicode{x2014}$learned simulators that predict the outcome of candidate actions in a compressed latent space before any is executed. Every predicted step must be computed on board within a tight power and thermal budget, yet the energy these steps fundamentally require, and how far real hardware sits from it, have not been characterized. We derive lower bounds on the arithmetic operations and the CMOS dynamic energy of a latent state transition from information theory and Boolean circuit complexity. Profiling a DreamerV3-style Recurrent State Space Model (RSSM) shows that every rollout operation is memory-bandwidth bound and that a GPU consumes at least $5.9\times10^{10}$ times the CMOS energy floor. Transient 3D thermal simulations show that this inefficiency is also a thermal one, since sustained back-to-back planning heats a millimeter-scale silicon tile to 556 K even behind an ideal copper heat spreader. Guided by this chain from bound to bottleneck, we propose the World Model Planning Accelerator (WMPA), a chip architecture for embedded world model planning that combines near-memory computation, real-time entropy estimation, variable-precision rollout units, and copy-on-write latent scratchpads. With a design target of 40x lower power, the same tile stays near ambient at 306 K in simulation, pointing toward practical planning on thermally constrained robots.

</details>

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

This paper analyzes the cooperative transportation of a payload suspended from two drones in which, instead of being attached to two independent ropes, the payload is allowed to slide along a single rope connecting both UAVs. First, a planar nondimensional dynamical model of the sliding motion with viscous friction is presented. Then, considering stationary drones, this configuration is compared with a fixed payload with the same total rope length, both at equilibrium and under disturbances. The analytical results show that the sliding payload equalizes the tension in both rope segments for any relative position of the drones, so that no rope loses tension at equilibrium; it doubles the region reachable by the payload and, except for low friction combined with a large drone separation, it enlarges the set of admissible disturbances. Finally, a dynamic simulation also shows that under realistic gusty wind profiles, the average tension imbalance between the drones is reduced with respect to the fixed-payload solution.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《$Σ$-LeWM: Learning World Models via S-Information for Robot Control》归入 机器人、世界模型、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；实时应用对效率提出要求。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、世界模型、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：PlaNet。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

World models have increasingly been applied as a model-based control method for robotics tasks such as manipulation and locomotion. While early world model architectures relied on decoders to prevent mode collapse and learn a predictable latent space, recent works on world models, such as Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture or LeWorldModel, increasingly aim to learn embeddings without a grounding decoder, utilising only a latent forward dynamics predictor and a stabilising entropy term. In this paper, we set out to demonstrate that the further introduction of two additional latent losses to LeWorldModel, a backward dynamics predictor and an inverse dynamics action predictor, enforces further structure on the latent embedding, leading to further gains in both performance and planning efficiency. These are critical properties for improving the applicability of such methods to real-time model-based control of real-world robots. We demonstrate these gains on standard world model benchmarks and two simulated robot manipulation tasks.

</details>

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

We examine what plan validation and execution reports establish in natural-language robot fleet control. Commands are traced through typed plans, deterministic corrections, fleet coordination and outcome checks in a three-level Robot Operating System 2 implementation with twenty simulated robots. An offline benchmark of 44 cases checks plan structure and selected command requirements. Across five local configurations without a response schema, 70.0-87.3% of responses pass. All runs in a selected 18-run simulation series meet their pose-based task conditions. This series includes two successful repeats that replaced incomplete runs after a software change. Saved plans and logs document duplicate pickup assignments and goal adjustments within the original task zones. Separate formation traces show a structurally accepted plan with the wrong formation shape and a rejected route, and an arrival report that omitted a requested robot after replanning. Carry completion records pickup and subsequent drop-off zone presence for the same robot. The cases distinguish changes to executable actions from changes to the requirements covered by a completion report. They motivate checking the effective assignments, goals and reported outcome against the original command.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《V3DSAM: Semantic-Geometric Loop Verification and Geometry-Anchored Persistent Instance Mapping for Feed-Forward Dense SLAM》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；使用优化建模或搜索过程。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；关注鲁棒性或泛化表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：ScanNet。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

To address unreliable cross-view association in long-sequence feed-forward dense SLAM, this letter presents V3DSAM, an open-vocabulary semantic dense SLAM system that establishes physical correspondences at both the scene and object levels. The system combines feed-forward geometric estimates from VGGT-SLAM 2.0 with open-vocabulary semantic observations from SAM3. We introduce semantic-geometric dual-scale loop verification, which identifies false loop closures using pixel-level bidirectional reprojection and object-level 3D spatial correspondences, without accessing backbone-internal features. We further propose geometry-anchored persistent instance mapping, which defines physical instances through class-constrained 3D connectivity in an optimized global coordinate system and assigns global IDs without inter-frame identity propagation. The proposed verifier rejects all four false loop candidates in our TUM RGB-D evaluation, while long-sequence ScanNet experiments show improved instance identity consistency. Thus, V3DSAM reduces dependence on internal backbone features and temporal ID propagation for robust long-term cross-view association.

</details>

- **归属领域**: 机器人
- **相关领域**: 机器人, 具身智能, 大模型
- **相关性评分**: 1.7（加权：具身智能 0.3，大模型 0.1，机器人 1.3）
- **关联关键词**: Multimodal, Robotics

#### 研究背景与动机

《UniOMA: Unified Optimal-Transport Multi-Modal Structural Alignment for Robot Perception》归入 机器人、具身智能、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：MultiBench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Achieving generalizable and well-aligned multimodal representations remains a core challenge in robot learning. While recent approaches have attempted to align modalities by modeling conditional or higher-order statistical dependencies, they do not explicitly consider the structural coherence across modalities. In this work, we propose a novel multimodal alignment method that augments existing contrastive losses with a geometry-aware Gromov--Wasserstein (GW) distance-based regularization. To this end, we encode intra-modal geometry for each modality and reduce cross-modal structural discrepancies through a dynamically learned GW barycenter, thereby encouraging structural consistency in addition to instance-level contrastive alignment. We apply this optimal-transport-based alignment strategy to robot perception tasks involving underexplored modalities such as force and tactile signals, where modality data often exhibit varying sample densities. Extensive experiments on five robotic benchmarks show that our method improves structural alignment and downstream performance over state-of-the-art multimodal contrastive baselines across a wide range of downstream tasks, including robot and environment state prediction. Moreover, our GW-based regularization can be seamlessly integrated into most InfoNCE-like objectives.

</details>

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

This paper focuses on the stochastic and real-time constraints of Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) path planning in Search and Rescue (SAR) missions. We introduce a configurable data-driven and intelligent routing architecture that combines a closed-loop probability update with the Random-Key Optimizers (RKO). Our problem is mathematically modeled as a dynamic probabilistic variant of the Open Vehicle Routing Problem (OVRP), enabling the solver to switch adaptively between two objectives, thereby balancing probability maximization and resource minimization. The framework degrades elegantly under noisy priors but not under biased ones: a systematically biased map performs about half as well as flying with no prior at all. Computational tests show that the proposed scheme is scalable and competitive with single-path scenarios in terms of solution performance and serves as a strong decision guide for intelligent planning in SAR operations in unstable environments

</details>

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Wearable robots depend on a software chain that links body-worn sensing to gait interpretation, assistance generation, and actuation. Personalization further requires each measured response to remain associated with the assistance parameters that generated it. This paper presents ExoStack, a modular ROS 2 framework for implementing that chain across embedded hubs and a host computer. Its Embedded Sensing and Actuation (ESA) library configures ESP32 boards as microROS sensing and actuation hubs. ExoCore supplies multi-hub synchronization, gait-event detection, profile generation, motion planning, cost evaluation, and human-in-the-loop (HIL) optimization as configuration-instantiated nodes. The PASE desktop application edits configurations, launches the processing stages, and visualizes ROS 2 topics. Hardware drivers and algorithm plugins can be added through defined interfaces without modifying the generic processing nodes. We characterize hub and planner timing on three embedded computers and demonstrate reuse on two pneumatic exosuits.

</details>

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；结果验证了方案可行性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Robots operating in the real world are inherently subject to partial observability, particularly in multi-robot collaboration, where individual observations may be insufficient for effective task execution. A key challenge is leveraging complementary observations from partner robots to support collaborative decision-making. We argue that information sharing should go beyond passively transmitting raw observations or generic visual features. Instead, robots should selectively encode task-relevant information according to task objectives and execution progress. To this end, we propose a task-oriented active context sharing framework that transforms robot observations into compact representations, preserving critical information under constrained communication budgets. The framework adopts a two-stage training paradigm. In the first stage, onboard observations and task instructions are jointly encoded into compact context representations. Task-oriented future prediction and soft task-progress correspondence learning guide this collaboration-agnostic compression, preserving information about task objectives and execution progress. In the second stage, the learned representations are integrated into a pretrained Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policy, enabling bidirectional context exchange. Each robot combines complementary partner information with local observations to guide action generation. Action supervision jointly refines the context representations and policy, aligning information sharing with collaborative decision-making. Controlled experiments demonstrate substantial performance gains under constrained communication budgets, while three real-world experiments validate the framework in complex dual-robot tasks. These results highlight a shift from passive visual transmission toward active, task-oriented context sharing that directly supports collaborative decision-making.

</details>

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Biological experimentation is not reducible to a single optimization problem, but requires decisions at multiple levels of abstraction: translating scientific knowledge into experimental procedures, adapting culture conditions to measured phenotypes and selecting interventions that test biological hypotheses. Automating experimental execution does not, by itself, determine which experiments should be performed next or how they should change in response to biological feedback. Here we introduce BioMARS, a human-in-the-loop multi-agent robotic system that connects knowledge-guided experimental decision-making with robotic execution and biological feedback. At the procedural level, BioMARS generated literature-grounded protocols and traceable action plans, improving expert-reviewed protocol quality scores across four model backends. At the parametric level, BioMARS used image-derived phenotypes from robotic retinal organoid culture to select subsequent experimental conditions, with higher reported scores in later rounds of the three-round experiment. At the intervention level, BioMARS nominated drug-repurposing candidates from biomedical evidence and advanced six candidates to wet-lab testing by researchers. Together, these studies demonstrate feedback-driven robotic biological experimentation within a framework that supports decisions about experimental procedures, culture conditions and biological interventions.

</details>
